# Every label defined in the Unravelled listings and memory maps, one per line:
#   NAME  ADDR  KIND  FILE(S) where it is defined (relative to this folder)
# KIND: entry = named ROM routine/data; local = Lxxxx label; code = code in RAM (e.g. GETNCH);
#       xref = only in an Unravelled symbol-table tab (FILE shows symtab:<tab>) (usually a label in another ROM's listing that the
#              listing itself leaves unlabelled); rmb = RAM variable (ADDR is its address); equ = constant (ADDR column is the VALUE).
# Spaces inside a name are shown as _ (e.g. BLOCK_6.0 is "BLOCK 6.0" in the source).
# A name can appear more than once if it differs between ROM versions (e.g. DECB 1.0 vs 1.1).
# Usage: grep -w DATPTR SYMBOLS.txt    or    grep " A01F " SYMBOLS.txt
